[^non-baroDa-CE] सुदृष्टां कुरु किष्किन्धां कुरुष्वात्मसमं पुरे ।  
क्रीडस्व च सह स्त्रीभिरहं ते दर्पशासनः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

सु दृष्टाम् कुरु किष्किन्धाम् कुरुष्व आत्म समम् पुरे ।
क्रीडयस्व च समम् स्त्रीभिः अहम् ते दर्प शासनः ॥ ४-११-३५
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘किष्किन्धापुरीको अच्छी तरह देख लो । अपने समान पुत्र आदिको इस नगरीके राज्यपर अभिषिक्त कर दो और स्त्रियोंके साथ आज जी भरकर क्रीडा कर लो । इसके बाद मैं तुम्हारा घमंड चूर कर दूँगा ॥ ३५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

किष्किन्धॆयन्नु चॆन्नागि नोडिकॊण्डु तन्नन्तॆ इरुव पुत्रनिगॆ राज्य सिंहासनदल्लि पट्टाभिषिक्तनन्नागिसु. इन्दु स्त्रीयरॊन्दिगॆ मनसोक्त क्रीडिसु. अनन्तर निन्न गर्ववन्नु नुच्चुनूरागिसुवॆनु.॥35॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*35. kiSkindhaam su dR^iSTaam kuru* = Kishkindha, clearly, seen city,
you make - see all Kishkindha as last-sight to your satisfaction; *pure aatma samam kuruSva*
= in city, your equalling one, be made - keep someone similar to you as in charge of city;
*striibhiH samam kriiDayasva* = females, along with, you rejoice; *aham te darpa
shaasanaH* = I am, your, egotism, destroyer.

**अनुवादः**

"Let Kishkindha city be seen clearly by you as last sight, and keep someone
equalling you as in charge of city, and also rejoice with the females till sunrise as there is no
tomorrow to you. [4-11-35]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Gaze well (_sudṛṣṭāṃ kuru_) upon Kiṣkindhā, appoint someone equal to yourself (_ātmasamam_) in the city, and sport together with your women—for I am the chastiser of your pride (_darpaśāsanaḥ_)!
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

यो हि मत्तं प्रमत्तं वा भग्नं वा रहितं कृशम् ।  
हन्यात् स भ्रूणहा लोके त्वद्विधं मदमोहितम् ॥ ३६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

यो हि मत्तं प्रमत्तं वा भग्नं वा रहितं कृशम् ।  
हन्यात् स भ्रूणहा लोके त्वद्विधं मदमोहितम् ॥ ३६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘जो मधुपानसे मत्त, प्रमत्त (असावधान), युद्धसे भगे हुए, अस्त्ररहित, दुर्बल, तुम्हारे-जैसे स्त्रियोंसे घिरे हुए तथा मदमोहित पुरुषका वध करता है, वह जगत् में गर्भ-हत्यारा कहा जाता है’ ॥ ३६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

मधुपानदिन्द मत्तनाद, प्रमत्त (जागरूकनल्लद)नाद, युद्धदिन्द ओडुत्तिरुव, अस्त्ररहित, दुर्बलनाद, निन्नन्तह स्त्रीयरिन्द सुत्तुवरिद मदमोहितनाद पुरुषन वधॆमाडुववनन्नु जगत्तिनल्लि भ्रूणहत्यॆ माडिदवनॆन्दु हेळुत्तारॆ.॥36॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*36. yaH* = he who is; *mattam* = drunken one; *pra mattam
vaa* = unvigilant one, or; *bhagnam vaa* = shattered - defeated one, or;
*rahitam* = one without [weapons]; *kR^isham* = atrophied one; *mada mohitam*
= by lust, lured one; *hanyaat* = if kills; *saH* = he; *loke* = in world;
*bhruuNa haa* = foeticide [sin of]; *tvat vidham* = your, way - your present state is
on par with those that are not to be killed.

**अनुवादः**

"He who kills a drunken one, unvigilant one, defeated one, or one without weapons,
or an atrophied one, he get the sin of foeticide in the world, and your present state is suchlike."
Dundubhi incited Vali in this way. [4-11-36]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

For whoever would slay one who is intoxicated (_mattam_), or heedless (_pramattam_), or broken (_bhagnam_), or unarmed (_rahitam_), or emaciated—one deluded by passion (_madamohitam_) like you—he would be a slayer of a Brahmin (_bhrūṇahā_) in the world!’
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Dismissing his women with a quiet laugh, Vālin declares his wine to be the heroic draught of battle, dons Indra's golden garland, seizes Dundubhi's horns, and hurls him down.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स प्रहस्याब्रवीन्मन्दं क्रोधात् तमसुरेश्वरम् ।  
विसृज्य ताः स्त्रियः सर्वास्ताराप्रभृतिकास्तदा ॥ ३७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स प्रहस्याब्रवीन्मन्दं क्रोधात् तमसुरेश्वरम् ।  
विसृज्य ताः स्त्रियः सर्वास्ताराप्रभृतिकास्तदा ॥ ३७ ॥
_________________
स प्रहस्याब्रवीन्मन्द क्रोधात्तमसुरेश्वरम् ।  
विसृज्य ताः स्त्रियः सर्वास्ताराप्रभृतिकास्तदा ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

स प्रहस्य अब्रवीत् मन्दम् क्रोधात् तम् असुरेश्वरम् ।
विसृज्य ताः स्त्रियः सर्वाः तारा प्रभृतिकाः तदा ॥ ४-११-३७
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘यह सुनकर वाली मन्द-मन्द मुसकराकर उन तारा आदि सब स्त्रियोंको दूर हटा उस असुरराजसे क्रोधपूर्वक बोला— ॥ ३७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

इदन्नु केळि मुगुळ्नक्कु वालियु तारॆये आदि स्त्रीयरन्नु दूरसरिसि आ असुरनल्लि क्रोधदिन्द हेळिदनु.॥37॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*37. tadaa saH pra hasya* = then, he, laughing off; *taaraa
prabhR^itikaaH taaH sarvaaH striyaH vi sR^ijya* = Tara, and others, them, all, women, on
discharging; *mandam* = to stupid one [Dundubhi]; *tam asura iishvaram* = to him,
demon, lord; *krodhaat* = in anger; *abraviit* = spoke.

**अनुवादः**

"Then Vali laughed that demon off, discharged all the females namely Tara and
others, and then he spoke to that stupid lord of demons in anger. [4-11-37]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Dismissing (_visṛjya_) all those women led by Tārā (_tārāprabhṛtikāḥ_) at that time, he laughed softly (_prahasyābravīn mandam_) in wrath and spoke to that lord of demons:
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

मत्तोऽयमिति मा मंस्था यद्यभीतोऽसि संयुगे ।  
मदोऽयं सम्प्रहारेऽस्मिन् वीरपानं समर्थ्यताम् ॥ ३८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

मत्तोऽयमिति मा मंस्था यद्यभीतोऽसि संयुगे ।  
मदोऽयं सम्प्रहारेऽस्मिन् वीरपानं समर्थ्यताम् ॥ ३८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

मत्तो अयम् इति मा मंस्था यदि अभीतो असि संयुगे ।
मदो अयम् संप्रहारे अस्मिन् वीर पानम् समर्थ्यताम् ॥ ४-११-३८
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘यदि तुम युद्धके लिये निर्भय होकर खड़े हो तो यह न समझो कि यह वाली मधु पीकर मतवाला हो गया है । मेरे इस मदको तुम युद्धस्थलमें उत्साहवृद्धिके लिये वीरोंद्वारा किया जानेवाला औषधविशेषका पान समझो’ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

नीनु युद्धक्कागि निर्भयवागि निन्तिद्दरॆ ई वालियु मधु कुडिदु उन्मत्तनागिद्दानॆ ऎन्दु तिळियबेड. नन्न ई मदवन्नु नीनु युद्धदल्लि उत्साह वृद्धिगागि वीररु सेविसुव औषधि विशेषवॆन्दु तिळि.॥38॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*38. ayam mattaH iti* = he is, drunk, thus; *maa maMsthaa* = do
not, surmise; *samyuge a bhiitaH asi yadi* = in fight, not, afraid, you are, if; *ayam
madaH* = this, drunkenness; *asmin sam prahaare* = in this, deadly fight; *viira
paanam samarthyataam* = as gallant's, toast, be regarded.

**अनुवादः**

"Don't surmise that I am drunk, and should you be unafraid of a fight, regard this
drunkenness of mine as the toast of a gallant fighter in this deadly fight." Vali said so to
Dundubhi. [4-11-38]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘Do not think (_mā maṃsthāḥ_), “This one is intoxicated (_matto &#x27;yam_),” if you are unafraid in battle! Let this intoxication be reckoned (_samarthyatām_) as the hero&#x27;s draught (_vīrapānam_) in this combat!’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तमेवमुक्त्वा सङ्क्रुद्धो मालामुत्क्षिप्य काञ्चनीम् ।  
पित्रा दत्तां महेन्द्रेण युद्धाय व्यवतिष्ठत ॥ ३९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तमेवमुक्त्वा सङ्क्रुद्धो मालामुत्क्षिप्य काञ्चनीम् ।  
पित्रा दत्तां महेन्द्रेण युद्धाय व्यवतिष्ठत ॥ ३९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तम् एवम् उक्त्वा संक्रुद्धो मालाम् उत्क्षिप्य कांचनीम् ।
पित्रा दत्ताम् महेन्द्रेण युद्धाय व्यवतिष्ठत ॥ ४-११-३९
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘उससे ऐसा कहकर पिता इन्द्रकी दी हुई विजयदायिनी सुवर्णमालाको गलेमें डालकर वाली कुपित हो युद्धके लिये खड़ा हो गया ॥ ३९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

अवनल्लि हीगॆ हेळि तन्दॆ इन्द्रनु कॊट्टिद्द विजयदायिनी सुवर्ण मालॆयन्नु कत्तिनल्लि धरिसि वालियु कुपितनागि युद्धक्कागि सिद्धनागि निन्तनु.॥39॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*39. tam evam uktvaa* = to him, that way, saying; *sam kruddhaH*
= becoming highly, enraged; *pitraa mahendreNa dattaam* = by father, by Mahendra given;
*maalaam utkSipya kaancaniim* = necklace [chest-pendant,] on heaving up, a golden one;
*yuddhaaya vyavatiSThata* = for fight, stood firm.

**अनुवादः**

"Saying that way to that Dundubhi that highly enraged Vali heaved up the golden
chest-pendant around his neck on to his chest, which was given by his father Mahendra, and stood
firm for fight. [4-11-39]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Having spoken thus to him in fierce wrath, lifting up the golden garland (_mālām utkṣipya kāñcanīm_) given by his father Mahendra, he stood ready for battle (_yuddhāya vyavatiṣṭhata_).
</details>
</details>

आविध्यत तथा वाली विनदन् कपिकुञ्जरः ॥ ४० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

विषाणयोर्गृहीत्वा तं दुन्दुभिं गिरिसन्निभम् ।

विषाणयोर्गृहीत्वा तं दुन्दुभिं गिरिसन्निभम् ।  
आविध्यत तदा वाली विनदन् कपिकुञ्जरः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

विषाणयो गृहीत्वा तम् दुन्दुभिम् गिरि संनिभम् ।
आविध्यत तथा वाली विनदन् कपि कुंजरः ॥ ४-११-४०
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘कपिश्रेष्ठ वालीने पर्वताकार दुन्दुभिके दोनों सींग पकड़कर उस समय गर्जना करते हुए उसे बारंबार घुमाया ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

कपिश्रेष्ठ वालियु पर्वताकार दुन्दुभिय ऎरडु कॊम्बुगळन्नु हिडिदु गर्जिसुत्ता अवनन्नु पदॆ-पदे तिरुगिसिदनु.॥40॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*40. tathaa* = then; *kapi kunjaraH* = monkey, the elephant;
*vaalii* = Vali; *giri sannibham* = mountain, similar; *tam dundubhim* = him,
that Dundubhi; *viSaaNayaH gR^ihiitvaa* = by horns, taking; *vi nadan* = highly,
booming; *aavidhyata* = [whirled and] bumped on ground.

**अनुवादः**

"Then that elephantine monkey Vali took that mountain-similar Dundubhi by horns, and
booming highly he whirled and bumped him onto ground. [4-11-40]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Seizing that mountain-like Dundubhi by both horns (_viṣāṇayor gṛhītvā_), Vālin, that elephant among monkeys (_kapikuñjaraḥ_), whirled him around (_āvidhyata_), roaring aloud;
</details>
</details>

बलाद् व्यापादयाञ्चक्रे ननर्द च महास्वनम् ।  
श्रोत्राभ्यामथ रक्तं तु तस्य सुस्राव पात्यतः ॥ ४१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

_________________
बलात् व्यापादयाञ्चक्रे ननर्द च महास्वनम् ।  
श्रोत्राभ्यामथ रक्तं तु तस्य सुस्राव पात्यतः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

वाली व्यापादयाम् चक्रे ननर्द च महास्वनम् ।
श्रोत्राभ्याम् अथ रक्तम् तु तस्य सुस्राव पात्यतः ॥ ४-११-४१
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘फिर बलपूर्वक उसे धरतीपर दे मारा और बड़े जोरसे सिंहनाद किया । पृथ्वीपर गिराये जाते समय उसके दोनों कानोंसे खूनकी धाराएँ बहने लगीं ॥ ४१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

मत्तॆ जोरागि अवनन्नु नॆलक्कॆ अप्पळिसि जोरागि सिंहनाद माडिदनु. नॆलक्कॆ अप्पळिसिदाग अवन ऎरडू किविगळिन्द रक्तद धारॆगळु हरियतॊडगिदवु.॥41॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*41. vaalii vyaapaadayaan [vi aa pat yaan] cakre* = Vali, to whirl and
throw on ground, he did; *na narda ca mahaa svanam* = verily, blaring, with great, sound;
*atha paatyataH* = then, while falling; *tasya shrotraabhyaam raktam su sraava* = of
his [Dundubhi's,] from two ears [of Dundubhi,] blood, gushed out.

**अनुवादः**

"Vali while blaring highly with great sound repeatedly whirled him and thrown onto
ground, and while Dundubhi was hurled and thrown to ground, blood gushed out of his two ears.
[4-11-41]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

with might he dashed him down (_vyāpādayāṃcakre_) and uttered a tremendous roar; and as he was being hurled down (_pātyataḥ_), blood gushed (_susrāva_) from both his ears (_śrotrābhyām_).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तयोस्तु क्रोधसंरम्भात् परस्परजयैषिणोः ।  
युद्धं समभवद् घोरं दुन्दुभेर्वालिनस्तथा ॥ ४२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] तयोस्तु क्रोधसंरम्भात् परस्परजयैषिणोः ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] युद्धं समभवद् घोरं दुन्दुभेर्वालिनस्तथा ॥ ४२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तयोः तु क्रोध संरम्भात् परस्पर जयैषिणोः ।
युद्धम् समभवत् घोरम् दुन्दुभेर् वालिनः तथा ॥ ४-११-४२
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘क्रोधके आवेशसे युक्त हो एक-दूसरेको जीतनेकी इच्छावाले उन दोनों दुन्दुभि और वालीमें घोर युद्ध होने लगा ॥ ४२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

क्रोधावेशदिन्द कूडि ऒब्बरु मत्तॊब्बरन्नु गॆल्ललु इच्छिसुव दुन्दुभि मत्तु वालि इवरल्लि घोर युद्ध नडॆयितु.॥42॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*42. krodha samrambhaat* = fury's, impetuosity; *paraspara jaya
iiSiNaH* = each other, victory, aspiring; *tayoH* = among two; *dundubheH vaalinaH
tathaa* = of Dundubhi, Vali, like that; *ghoram yuddham sam abhavat* = gruesome fight,
occurred.

**अनुवादः**

"There occurred a gruesome fight among those two, Dundubhi and Vali, who by their
fury are impetuous and who aspired victory over the other. [4-11-42]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Then, out of the fury of their wrath (_krodhasaṃrambhāt_), a dreadful battle arose between those two—Dundubhi and Vālin—each eager for victory over the other (_parasparajayaiṣiṇoḥ_).
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Fighting with fists, knees, boulders, and trees, Vālin crushes Dundubhi to death, hefts his lifeless body, and hurls it a full yojana away, scattering blood drops into Mataṅga's hermitage.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अयुध्यत तदा वाली शक्रतुल्यपराक्रमः ।  
मुष्टिभिर्जानुभिः पद्भिः शिलाभिः पादपैस्तथा ॥ ४३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] अयुध्यत तदा वाली शक्रतुल्यपराक्रमः ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] मुष्टिभिर्जानुभिः पद्भिः शिलाभिः पादपैस्तथा ॥ ४३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

अयुध्यत तदा वाली शक्र तुल्य पराक्रमः ।
मुष्टिभिर् जानुभिः पद्भिः शिलाभिः पादपैः तथा ॥ ४-११-४३
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘उस समय इन्द्रके तुल्य पराक्रमी वाली दुन्दुभिपर मुक्कों, लातों, घुटनों, शिलाओं तथा वृक्षोंसे प्रहार करने लगा ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

आग इन्द्रतुल्य पराक्रमि वालियु दुन्दुभिगॆ मुष्टिगळिन्द, ऒदॆगळिन्द, वृक्षगळिन्द, शिलॆगळिन्द प्रहरिसतॊडगिदनु.॥43॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*43. tadaa* = then; *shakra tulya paraakramaH* = Indra, similar,
in fortitude; *vaalii* = Vali; *muSTibhiH jaanubhiH padbhiH* = with fists, knees,
feet; *tathaa* = like that; shilaabhiH paadapaiH with boulders, trees; *a yudhyata* =
fought.

**अनुवादः**

"Then Vali who is similar to Indra in his fortitude fought with him with his fists,
knees and feet, and like that with boulders and trees. [4-11-43]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Then Vālin, equal in valor to Śakra (_śakratulyaparākramaḥ_), fought with his fists, knees, and feet (_muṣṭibhir jānubhiḥ padbhiḥ_), as well as with boulders and trees.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

परस्परं घ्नतोस्तत्र वानरासुरयोस्तदा ।  
आसीद्धीनोऽसुरो युद्धे शक्रसूनुर्व्यवर्धत ॥ ४४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] परस्परं घ्नतोस्तत्र वानरासुरयोस्तदा ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] आसीद्धीनोऽसुरो युद्धे शक्रसूनुर्व्यवर्धत ॥ ४४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

परस्परम् घ्नतोः तत्र वानर असुरयोः तदा ।
आसीत् हीनो असुरो युद्धे शक्र सूनुः व्यवर्धत ॥ ४-११-४४
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘उस युद्धस्थलमें परस्पर प्रहार करते हुए वानर और असुर दोनों योद्धाओंमेंसे असुरकी शक्ति तो घटने लगी और इन्द्रकुमार वालीका बल बढ़ने लगा ॥ ४४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

आ युद्धदल्लि परस्पर प्रहार माडुत्ता वानर मत्तु असुर इब्बरू योधरल्लि असुरन शक्ति क्षिणिसतॊडगितु, इन्द्रकुमार वालिय बल हॆच्चतॊडगितु.॥44॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*44. tadaa tatra* = then, there; *vaanara asurayoH* = of monkey,
demon; *parasparam ghnatoH* = each other, assaulting; *yuddhe asuraH hiinaH aasiit* =
in fight, demon, impaired, he became; *shakra suunuH vyavardhata* = Indra's, son,
toughened.

**अनुवादः**

"While each is assaulting the other in that fight between that monkey and demon,
that demon is impaired and Indra's son Vali toughened. [4-11-44]

**टिप्पनी**

Vali is wearing the golden chest-pendent given by his father Indra, wearing
which depletes half of the opponent's strength. Hence, the strength of Dundubhi is depleted now.
Vali does not wear the same chest pendent when fighting with Sugreeva when killed by Rama. For this
it is said, Vali wanted to punish Sugreeva that which is befitting to an errant younger brother,
but Vali never considered Sugreeva as a real threat or an enemy to the core.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

As the monkey and the demon smote each other (_parasparaṃ ghnatoḥ_) there at that time, the demon grew exhausted (_āsīd dhīnaḥ_) in the fray while the son of Śakra waxed mightier (_vyavardhata_)!
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तं तु दुन्दुभिमुद्यम्य धरण्यामभ्यपातयत् ।  
युद्धे प्राणहरे तस्मिन्निष्पिष्टो दुन्दुभिस्तदा ॥ ४५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] तं तु दुन्दुभिमुद्यम्य धरण्यामभ्यपातयत् ।  
युद्धे प्राणहरे तस्मिन्निष्पिष्टो दुन्दुभिस्तदा ॥ ४५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तम् तु दुन्दुभिम् उद्यम्य धरण्याम् अभ्यपातयत् ।
युद्धे प्राणहरे तस्मिन् निष्पिष्टो दुन्दुभिः तदा ॥ ४-११-४५
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘उन दोनोंमें वहाँ प्राणान्तकारी युद्ध छिड़ गया । उस समय वालीने दुन्दुभिको उठाकर पृथ्वीपर दे मारा, साथ ही अपने शरीरसे उसको दबा दिया, जिससे दुन्दुभि पिस गया ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

अवरिब्बरल्लि अल्लि प्राणान्तकारी युद्ध हत्तिकॊण्डितु. आग वालियु दुन्दुभियन्नु ऎत्ति नॆलक्कॆ ऒगॆदु, जॊतॆगॆ तन्न शरीरदिन्द अवनन्नु ऒत्तिबिट्टाग दुन्दुभियु नुच्चुनूरादनु.॥45॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*45. tam dundubhim* = him, Dundubhi is; *udyamya dharaNyaam
abhyapaatayat [abhi aa paatayat]* = on lifting, on to ground, flung him; *tadaa praaNa hare
tasmin yuddhe* = then, life, taking, in that, fight; *dundubhiH* = Dundubhi is; *niS
piSTaH* = completely pounded out.

**अनुवादः**

"In that life-taking fight when Dundubhi is lifted up and flung to ground, he is
completely pounded out. [4-11-45]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Lifting up (_udyamya_) that Dundubhi, he hurled him down (_abhyapātayat_) upon the earth; in that life-destroying combat, Dundubhi was crushed to pulp (_niṣpiṣṭaḥ_) at that time.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स्रोतोभ्यो बहु रक्तं तु तस्य सुस्राव पात्यतः ।  
पपात च महाबाहुः क्षितौ पञ्चत्वमागतः ॥ ४६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] स्रोतोभ्यो बहु रक्तं तु तस्य सुस्राव पात्यतः ।

_________________
स्रोतेभ्यो बहुरक्तं तु तस्य सुस्राव पात्यतः ।  
पपात च महाबाहुः क्षितौ पञ्चत्वमागतः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

स्रोत्रेभ्यो बहु रक्तम् तु तस्य सुस्राव पात्यतः ।
पपात च महाबाहुः क्षितौ पंचत्वम् आगतः ॥ ४-११-४६
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘गिरते समय उसके शरीरके समस्त छिद्रोंसे बहुत-सा रक्त बहने लगा । वह महाबाहु असुर पृथ्वीपर गिरा और मर गया ॥ ४६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

बीळुवाग अवन शरीरद ऎल्ल द्वारगळिन्द हॆच्चागि रक्त हरियतॊडगितु. आ महाबाहु असुरनु भूमिगॆ बिद्दु सत्तुहोदनु.॥46॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*46. paatyataH tasya srotrebhyaH* = while he is felled, from his
vent-holes -ears, nose etc; *bahu raktam su sraava* = much, blood, much flown;
*mahaabaahuH pancatvam aagataH kSitau pa paata* = great-shouldered one [mighty one,]
fifth-stated, has come [attained.] and fell, on ground.

**अनुवादः**

"While he is felled down much blood is flown out from the vent-holes of his body,
nine of them, ears, nose, eyes etc., and on his falling that mighty one Dundubhi attained the
fifth-state. [4-11-46]

**टिप्पनी**

The 'fifth state' of a living being is death, where the other four states are [1
* jaagR^it avastha,* waking state, [2 * swapna avastha,* dreaming state, [3
*suSupta avastha,* deep sleep, [4 * turriya avastha,* fourth
state, that which is above the three preceding states. Then the fifth state is death.
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

From the orifices (_srotobhyaḥ_) of him as he was dashed down, much blood gushed forth; and the mighty-armed one fell upon the ground, having met his death (_pañcatvam āgataḥ_).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तं तोलयित्वा बाहुभ्यां गतसत्त्वमचेतनम् ।  
चिक्षेप वेगवान् वाली वेगेनैकेन योजनम् ॥ ४७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तं तोलयित्वा बाहुभ्यां गतसत्त्वमचेतनम् ।  
चिक्षेप वेगवान् वाली वेगेनैकेन योजनम् ॥ ४७ ॥
_________________
तं तोलयित्वा बाहुभ्यां गतसत्त्वमचेतनम् ।  
चिक्षेप बलवान्वाली वेगेनैकेन योजनम् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तम् तोलयित्वा बाहुभ्याम् गत सत्त्वम् अचेतनम् ।
चिक्षेप वेगवान् वाली वेगेन एकेन योजनम् ॥ ४-११-४७
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘जब उसके प्राण निकल गये और चेतना लुप्त हो गयी, तब वेगवान् वालीने उसे दोनों हाथोंसे उठाकर एक साधारण वेगसे एक योजन दूर फेंक दिया ॥ ४७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

वन प्राणगळु हॊरटु होदाग चैतन्य उडुगि होयितु. आग वेगवन्त वालियु अवनन्नु ऎरडु कैगळिन्द ऎत्ति सामान्य वेगदिन्द ऒन्दु योजन दूरक्कॆ ऎसॆदुबिट्टनु.॥47॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*47. vegavaan vaalii* = hastiest one, Vali; *gata sattvam* = he
who lost, stamina; *a cetanam* = inanimate one; *tam baahubhyaam tolayitvaa* = him,
with both arms, by swaying; *ekena vegena yojanam cikSepa* = in a single, flick, to a yojana
length, hurled.

**अनुवादः**

"Then the hastiest Vali swayed that dead and inanimate demon with both of his hands
and hurled him a yojana distance in a single flick. [4-11-47]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Hefting (_tolayitvā_) with both arms that lifeless, unconscious (_gatasattvam acetanam_) body, the swift Vālin hurled (_cikṣepa_) it a full _yojana_ (_yojanam_) with a single impulse (_vegenaikena_)!
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तस्य वेगप्रविद्धस्य वक्त्रात् क्षतजबिन्दवः ।  
प्रपेतुर्मारुतोत्क्षिप्ता मतङ्गस्याश्रमं प्रति ॥ ४८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तस्य वेगप्रविद्धस्य वक्त्रात् क्षतजबिन्दवः ।  
प्रपेतुर्मारुतोत्क्षिप्ता मतङ्गस्याश्रमं प्रति ॥ ४८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तस्य वेग प्रविद्धस्य वक्त्रात् क्षतज बिन्दवः ।
प्रपेतुः मारुत उत्क्षिप्ता मतंगस्य आश्रमम् प्रति ॥ ४-११-४८
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘वेगपूर्वक फेंके गये उस असुरके मुखसे निकली हुई रक्तकी बहुत-सी बूँदें हवाके साथ उड़कर मतंगमुनिके आश्रममें पड़ गयीं ॥ ४८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

वेगवागि ऎसॆद आ असुरन बायियिन्द हॊरट रक्तद अनेक बिन्दुगळु गाळियल्लि हारि मतङ्ग मुनिय आश्रमदल्लि बिद्दवु.॥48॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*48. vega pra viddhasya* = hastily, well, thrown one; *tasya* =
his [Dundubhi's]; *vaktraat kSataja bindavaH* = from mouth, fallen, drops [of blood];
*maaruta utkSiptaa* = by air, uplifted; *matangasya aashramam prati* = Sage
Matanga's, hermitage, towards pra petuH = fallen.

**अनुवादः**

"And while he is thrown that hastily blood drops oozed from his mouth, and flung by
air they fell in the hermitage of sage Matanga. [4-11-48]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

From the mouth of him who had been hurled with such force (_vegapraviddhasya_), drops of blood (_kṣatajabindavaḥ_), tossed aloft by the wind (_mārutotkṣiptāḥ_), fell toward the hermitage of Mataṅga (_mataṅgasyāśramaṃ prati_).
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Defiled by the falling drops of blood and beholding the slain buffalo-demon, Sage Mataṅga discerns Vālin's deed through his ascetic power and pronounces a mortal curse upon him.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तान् दृष्ट्वा पतितांस्तत्र मुनिः शोणितविप्रुषः ।  
क्रुद्धस्तस्य महाभाग चिन्तयामास को न्वयम् ॥ ४९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तान् दृष्ट्वा पतितांस्तत्र मुनिः शोणितविप्रुषः ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] क्रुद्धस्तस्य महाभाग चिन्तयामास को न्वयम् ॥ ४९ ॥
_________________
तान् दृष्ट्वा पतितांस्तत्र मुनिः शोणितविप्रुषः ।  
क्रुद्धस्तत्र महाभाग चिन्तयामास को न्वयम् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तान् दृष्ट्वा पतिताम् तत्र मुनिः शोणित विप्रुषः ।
क्रुद्धः तस्य महाभाग चिन्तयामास कोन्वम् ॥ ४-११-४९
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘महाभाग! वहाँ पड़े हुए उन रक्त-बिन्दुओंको देखकर मतंगमुनि कुपित हो उठे और इस विचारमें पड़ गये कि ‘यह कौन है, जो यहाँ रक्तके छींटे डाल गया है? ॥ ४९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

महाभागने! अल्लि बिद्दिरुव रक्तद बिन्दुगळन्नु नोडि मतङ्ग मुनियु कुपितरागि ‘इल्लि रक्तद बिन्दुगळन्नु चॆल्लिदवनु यारु?’ ऎम्ब विचारदल्लि बिद्दरु.॥49॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*49. mahaabhaaga* = oh, great opportune one [Rama]; *muniH* =
the sage; *tatra patitaam* = there fallen; *taan* = them; *shoNita vipruSaH* =
blood, drops; *dR^iSTvaa* = on seeing; *tasya* = of him - on the one who dropped
blood; *kruddhaH* = enraged; *ayam kaH nu* = he, who is, indeed [thus as];
*cintayaamaasa* = thought of.

**अनुवादः**

"On seeing the blood drops fallen there, oh Rama, that sage is enraged and thought,
'who is he indeed, who dropped the blood...' Thus Sugreeva narrated to Rama. [4-11-49]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Seeing those drops of blood (_śoṇitavipruṣaḥ_) fallen there, the illustrious sage grew wrathful (_kruddhaḥ_) with him and reflected (_cintayāmāsa_): ‘Who indeed is this—
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

येनाहं सहसा स्पृष्टः शोणितेन दुरात्मना ।  
कोऽयं दुरात्मा दुर्बुद्धिरकृतात्मा च बालिशः ॥ ५० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] येनाहं सहसा स्पृष्टः शोणितेन दुरात्मना ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] कोऽयं दुरात्मा दुर्बुद्धिरकृतात्मा च बालिशः ॥ ५० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

येन अहम् सहसा स्पृष्टः शोणितेन दुरात्मना ।
कोऽयम् दुरात्मा दुर् बुद्धिः अकृतात्मा च बालिशः ॥ ४-११-५०
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘जिस दुष्टने सहसा मेरे शरीरसे रक्तका स्पर्श करा दिया, यह दुरात्मा दुर्बुद्धि, अजितात्मा और मूर्ख कौन है?’ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

याव दुष्टनु नन्न शरीरक्कॆ रक्तवन्नु स्पर्शिसिदनो, आ दुरात्मा, दुर्बुद्धि, अजितात्मा, मूर्खयारागिद्दानॆ.॥50॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*50. yena* = by which; *dur aatmanaa* = evil-minded one;
*aham sahasaa spR^iSTaH shoNitena* = I am, abruptly, touched, by blood; *dur aatmaa*
= evil-minded; *dur buddhiH* = evil-minded one [malevolent one]; *a kR^ita atmaa* =
disobedient one; *kaH ayam baalishaH* = who is, this, child-like [reckless one.]

**अनुवादः**

" 'By which evil-spirited one I am abruptly touched with blood? Who is that
evil-minded one? Who is that malevolent, disobedient and a reckless one?' Thus the sage Matanga
pondered. [4-11-50]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

by which wicked-souled one (_durātmanā_) I have suddenly been spattered with blood (_spṛṣṭaḥ śoṇitena_)? Who is this evil-minded, perverse, unchastened (_akṛtātmā_), and childish fool (_bāliśaḥ_)?’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

इत्युक्त्वा स विनिष्क्रम्य ददृशे मुनिसत्तमः ।  
महिषं पर्वताकारं गतासुं पतितं भुवि ॥ ५१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] इत्युक्त्वा स विनिष्क्रम्य ददृशे मुनिसत्तमः ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] महिषं पर्वताकारं गतासुं पतितं भुवि ॥ ५१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

इति उक्त्वा स विनिष्क्रम्य ददृशे मुनिसत्तम ।
महिषम् पर्वत आकारम् गत असुम् पतितम् भुवि ॥ ४-११-५१
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘ऐसा कहकर मुनिवर मतंगने बाहर निकलकर देखा तो उन्हें एक पर्वताकार भैंसा पृथ्वीपर प्राणहीन होकर पड़ा दिखायी दिया ॥ ५१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

हीगॆ हेळि मुनिवर मतङ्गरु हॊरगॆ बन्दु नोडिदरॆ अवरिगॆ ऒन्दु पर्वतदन्तह कोणनु प्राणहीननागि नॆलदल्लि बिद्दिरुवुदन्नु नोडिदरु.॥51॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*51. saH muni sattama* = he, that sage, celebrated one; *iti uktvaa
vi niSkramya* = thus, said [thinking,] verily, exiting [from hermitage, coming out]; *gata
asum* = gone, lives [lifeless]; *bhuvi patitam* = on ground, fallen; *parvata
aakaaram mahiSam dadR^ishe* = mountain, in shape, buffalo, he has seen,

**अनुवादः**

"Thinking thus and coming out of hermitage that celebrated sage has seen the
mountainous buffalo fallen on ground lifelessly. [4-11-51]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Speaking thus and stepping forth (_viniṣkramya_), that best of sages beheld the mountain-sized buffalo lying lifeless (_gatāsum_) upon the ground.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स तु विज्ञाय तपसा वानरेण कृतं हि तत् ।  
उत्ससर्ज महाशापं क्षेप्तारं वानरं प्रति ॥ ५२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] स तु विज्ञाय तपसा वानरेण कृतं हि तत् ।

[^non-baroDa-CE] स तु विज्ञाय तपसा वानरेण कृतं हि तत् ।  
उत्ससर्ज महाशापं क्षेप्तारं वालिनं प्रति ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘उन्होंने अपने तपोबलसे यह जान लिया कि यह एक वानरकी करतूत है । अतः उस लाशको फेंकनेवाले वानरके प्रति उन्होंने बड़ा भारी शाप दिया— ॥ ५२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

अवरु तम्म तपो बलदिन्द इदॊन्दु वानरन कृत्यवागिदॆ ऎन्दु तिळिदु कॊण्डरु. आद्दरिन्द आ हॆणवन्नु ऎसॆदिरुव वानरन कुरितु अवरु भारी शापवन्नु कॊट्टरु.॥52॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*52. saH tat* = he, that [deed]; *vaanareNa kR^itam* = by
monkey, done; *tapasaa vij~naaya* = by ascetic power, on knowing; *kSeptaaram vaanaram
prati* = who tossed, that monkey, towards him; *mahaa shaapam* = great, curse;
*utsasarja* = released.

**अनुवादः**

"On knowing by his ascetic power that this deed is done by the monkey, he released a
great curse on him who tossed the cadaver of buffalo. [4-11-52]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Discerning through his ascetic power (_vijñāya tapasā_) that this had been done by a monkey, he uttered a mighty curse (_mahāśāpam_) against the monkey who had hurled (_kṣeptāram_) it:
</details>
</details>

वनं मत्संश्रयं येन दूषितं रुधिरस्रवैः ॥ ५३ ॥

इह तेनाप्रवेष्टव्यं प्रिविष्टस्य वधो भवेत् ।

</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

इह तेन अप्रवेष्टव्यम् प्रविष्टस्य वधो भवेत् ।
वनम् मत् संश्रयम् येन दूषितम् रुधिर स्रवैः ॥ ४-११-५३
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘जिसने खूनके छींटे डालकर मेरे निवासस्थान इस वनको अपवित्र कर दिया है, वह आजसे इस वनमें प्रवेश न करे । यदि इसमें प्रवेश करेगा तो उसका वध हो जायगा ॥ ५३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

यावनु रक्तद बिन्दुगळन्नु हाकि नन्न आश्रम वन्नु, ई वनवन्नु अपवित्रनागिसिदनो, अवनु इन्दिनिन्द ई वनदल्लि प्रवेशिसदिरलि. इदरल्लि प्रवेशिसिदरॆ अवनु सत्तु होगुवनु.॥53॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*53. mat samshrayam vanam* = my, sheltering, woods; *yena* = by
whom; *rudhira sravaiH duuSitam* = by blood, squirts of, is stained; *tena* = by him;
*iha* = in here; *a praveSTavyam* = not, enterable; *praviSTasya vadhaH
bhavet* = if enters, fatality, occurs.

**अनुवादः**

"Untreadable is this sheltering wood of mine to him who has stained it with squirts
of blood, and if he enters this place peradventure, fatality occurs on him. [4-11-53]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘This forest, my refuge (_matsaṃśrayam_), must not be entered by him (_tenāpraveṣṭavyam_) by whom it has been defiled with streams of blood (_rudhirasravaiḥ_); should he enter, his death shall ensue (_praviṣṭasya vadho bhavet_)—
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

क्षिपता पादपाश्चेमे सम्भग्नाश्चासुरीं तनुम् ।  
समन्तादाश्रमं पूर्णं योजनं मामकं यदि ॥ ५४ ॥  
आगमिष्यति दुर्बुद्धिर्व्यक्तं स न भविष्यति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] क्षिपता पादपाश्चेमे सम्भग्नाश्चासुरीं तनुम् ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] समन्तादाश्रमं पूर्णं योजनं मामकं यदि ॥ ५४ ॥  
[^non-baroDa-CE] आगमिष्यति दुर्बुद्धिर्व्यक्तं स न भविष्यति ।
_________________
[^non-baroDa-CE] क्षिपता पादपाश्चेमे सम्भग्नाश्चासुरीं तनुम् ।  
समन्तादाश्रमं पूर्णं योजन मामकं यदि ॥  
[^non-baroDa-CE] आगमिष्यति दुर्बुद्धिर्व्यक्तं स न भविष्यति ।
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

क्षिपता पादपाः च इमे संभग्नाः च असुरीम् तनुम् ।
समन्तात् आश्रमम् पूर्णम् योजनम् मामकम् यदि ॥ ४-११-५४
आगमिष्यति दुर्बुद्धिः व्यक्तम् स न भविष्यति ।
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘इस असुरके शरीरको इधर फेंककर जिसने इन वृक्षोंको तोड़ डाला है, वह दुर्बुद्धि यदि मेरे आश्रमके चारों ओर पूरे एक योजनतककी भूमिमें पैर रखेगा तो अवश्य ही अपने प्राणोंसे हाथ धो बैठेगा ॥ ५४ १/२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

ई असुरन शरीरवन्नु इत्त ऎसॆदु यावनु ई वृक्षगळन्नु मुरिदुहाकिरुवनो, आ दुर्बुद्धियु नन्न आश्रमद सुत्तलु ऒन्दु योजनदवरॆगिन भूमियल्लि कालिट्टरॆ अवश्यवागि प्राणगळन्नु कळॆदुकॊळ्ळुवनु.॥54½॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*54, 55a. asuriim tanum kshipataa* = demon's, body, by him who hurled;
*ime paadapaaH ca sam bhagnaaH* = these trees, also, completely destroyed; *samantaat
puurNam yojanam* = around, full, one yojana; *maamakam aashramam aagamiSyati yadi* =
mine, hermitage, he places foot, if; *durbuddhiH* = evil-minded; *saH na bhaviSyati
vyaktam* = he, will not, exists, evidently.

**अनुवादः**

"By his hurling the demon's body these trees are also completely destroyed, hence he
ought not set his foot in a distance of one full yojana around this hermitage, and if that
evil-minded one places his foot then he evidently does not exist. [4-11-54, 55a]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

and by whom, in hurling this demon&#x27;s carcass (_kṣipatā... āsurīṃ tanum_), these trees have been shattered—if within a full _yojana_ all around my hermitage (_samantād āśramaṃ pūrṇaṃ yojanam_)—
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Mataṅga decrees that any retainer of Vālin remaining in his grove past sunset shall turn to stone for thousands of years, prompting the monkeys to flee to Vālin.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ये चास्य सचिवाः केचित् संश्रिता मामकं वनम् ॥ ५५ ॥  
न च तैरिह वस्तव्यं श्रुत्वा यान्तु यथासुखम् ।  
तेऽपि वा यदि तिष्ठन्ति शपिष्ये तानपि ध्रुवम् ॥ ५६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] ये चास्य सचिवाः केचित् संश्रिता मामकं वनम् ॥ ५५ ॥  
[^non-baroDa-CE] न च तैरिह वस्तव्यं श्रुत्वा यान्तु यथासुखम् ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] तेऽपि वा यदि तिष्ठन्ति शपिष्ये तानपि ध्रुवम् ॥ ५६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘उस वालीके जो कोई सचिव भी मेरे इस वनमें रहते हों, उन्हें अब यहाँका निवास त्याग देना चाहिये । वे मेरी आज्ञा सुनकर सुखपूर्वक यहाँसे चले जायँ । यदि वे रहेंगे तो उन्हें भी निश्चय ही शाप दे दूँगा ॥ ५५-५६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

आ वालिय यावने सचिवनू कूड नन्न ई वनदल्लिद्दरॆ, अवनू इल्लिन्द हॊरटुहोगबेकु. अवरु नन्न आज्ञॆयन्नु केळि सुखवागि इल्लिन्द हॊरटु होगलि. अवरु इल्लिये इद्दरॆ निश्चयवागि अवरिगॆ शापकॊडुवॆनु.॥55-56॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*55b, 56a. maamakam vanam samshritaa* = belonging to me, woods,
depending on; *ye kechit asya sachivaaH* = those, few, his, ministers [friends] are there;
*taiH ca* = by them, also; *iha na vastavya* = here, not, to live; *shrutvaa*
= on hearing - my word; *yaantu yathaa sukham* = let them go, as for, comfortably - solace
themselves with my mercy.

**अनुवादः**

"And some of his friends that are dependent on my woods shall not live here and they
may depart on hearing my words and solace themselves with my words. [4-11-55b, 56a]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**55:** ‘—that perverse-minded one (_durbuddhiḥ_) should come, manifestly he shall cease to exist (_vyaktaṃ sa na bhaviṣyati_)! And whatever followers (_sacivāḥ_) of his have resorted to my forest,

**56:** they too must not dwell here; having heard this, let them depart at their ease (_yathāsukham_). Or if they remain here, I shall assuredly curse them as well (_śapiṣye tān api dhruvam_)!
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

वनेऽस्मिन् मामके नित्यं पुत्रवत् परिरक्षिते ।  
पत्राङ्कुरविनाशाय फलमूलाभवाय च ॥ ५७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] वनेऽस्मिन् मामके नित्यं पुत्रवत् परिरक्षिते ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] पत्राङ्कुरविनाशाय फलमूलाभवाय च ॥ ५७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘मैंने अपने इस वनकी सदा पुत्रकी भाँति रक्षा की है । जो इसके पत्र और अङ्कुरका विनाश तथा फल-मूलका अभाव करनेके लिये यहाँ रहेंगे, वे अवश्य शापके भागी होंगे ॥ ५७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

नानु नन्न ई वनवन्नु सदा मक्कळन्तॆ रक्षिसिरुवॆनु. यावनु इदर ऎलॆ, चिगुरु कित्तु, फल-मूलगळन्नु अभावगॊळिसि इल्ले इद्दरॆ अवनू अवश्यवागि शापक्कॆ गुरियागुवनु.॥57॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*56b, 57. nityam putravat pari rakSate* = always, son-like, protected;
*maamake asmin vane* = belonging to me, in this, forest; *te api* = they, even;
*patra ankura vinaashaaya* = leaves, sprouts, for destruction; *phala muula a bhavaaya ca
vaa* = fruits, tubers, for non-existence, also, or; *tiSTanti yadi* = stay behind, if;
*taan api shapiSye dhruvam* = them, even, I curse, definitely.

**अनुवादः**

"This forest of mine is always protected like my own son, and if the monkeys of Vali
wish to stay behind in this forest alone for further destruction of leaves or sprouts, or even for
the non-existence of fruits and tubers of this forest, defiantly they too will be cursed.
[4-11-57]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

In this forest of mine, ever cherished like a son (_putravat parirakṣite_), [they dwell] to the destruction of leaves and shoots (_patrāṅkuravināśāya_) and the ruin of fruits and roots (_phalamūlābhavāya ca_).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

दिवसश्चाद्य मर्यादा यं द्रष्टा श्वोऽस्मि वानरम् ।  
बहुवर्षसहस्राणि स वै शैलो भविष्यति ॥ ५८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] दिवसश्चाद्य मर्यादा यं द्रष्टा श्वोऽस्मि वानरम् ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] बहुवर्षसहस्राणि स वै शैलो भविष्यति ॥ ५८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

दिवसः च अद्य मर्यादा यम् द्रष्टा श्वः अस्मि वानरम् ।
बहु वर्ष सहस्राणि स वै शैलः भविष्यति ॥ ४-११-५८
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘आजका दिन उन सबके आने-जाने या रहनेकी अन्तिम अवधि है—आजभरके लिये मैं उन सबको छुट्टी देता हूँ । कलसे जो कोई वानर यहाँ मेरी दृष्टिमें पड़ जायगा, वह कई हजार वर्षोंके लिये पत्थर हो जायगा’ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

इन्दिन दिवस अवरॆल्लरिगॆ बन्दु-होगुव अथवा इरुव कॊनॆय अवधियागिदॆ. इन्दिन ऒन्दु दिन अवरिगॆ सवलत्तु कॊडुत्तेनॆ. नाळॆयिन्द यावने वानरनु नन्न कण्णिगॆ बिद्दरॆ, अवनु अनेक साविर वर्ष कल्लागुवनु.॥58॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*58. adya divasaH maryaadaa* = today, is the day, of limit; *shvaH
yam vaanaram* = tomorrow, which, monkey; *draSTaa asmi* = I will see; *saH* =
that monkey; *bahu varSa sahasraaNi* = for many, years, thousands to come; *shailaH
bhaviSyati vai* = he, stone, he becomes, [petrified.] indeed.

**अनुवादः**

"And today is the day of limit and the monkey whom I will see tomorrow, he will be
petrified for many thousand years to come. [4-11-58]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Today&#x27;s daytime is the limit (_divasaś cādya maryādā_): whatever monkey I shall behold tomorrow (_śvaḥ_), he shall assuredly become a rock (_śailo bhaviṣyati_) for many thousands of years!’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ततस्ते वानराः श्रुत्वा गिरं मुनिसमीरिताम् ।  
निश्चक्रमुर्वनात् तस्मात् तान् दृष्ट्वा वालिरब्रवीत् ॥ ५९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] ततस्ते वानराः श्रुत्वा गिरं मुनिसमीरिताम् ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] निश्चक्रमुर्वनात् तस्मात् तान् दृष्ट्वा वालिरब्रवीत् ॥ ५९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

ततः ते वानराः श्रुत्वा गिरम् मुनि समीरिताम् ।
निश्चक्रमुः वनात् तस्मात् तान् दृष्ट्वा वालिर् अब्रवीत् ॥ ४-११-५९
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘मुनिके इस वचनको सुनकर वे सभी वानर मतंगवनसे निकल गये । उन्हें देखकर वालीने पूछा— ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

मुनिय ई मातन्नु केळि आ ऎल्ल वानररु मतङ्गवनदिन्द हॊरटुहोदरु. अवरन्नु कण्डु वालियु केळिदनु.॥59॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*59. tataH te vaanaraaH* = then, they, monkeys; *muni sam iiritaam
giram shrutvaa* = by sage, clearly, said, words, on hearing; *tasmaat vanaat niH
chakramuH* = from that, forest, to out, they started; *taan dR^iSTvaa vaaliH abraviit* =
them, on seeing, Vali, spoke.

**अनुवादः**

"Then those monkeys on hearing the clear wording of the sage started out from that
forest, and on their coming to Kishkindha, Vali saw them and spoke this way to them. [4-11-59]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Then, hearing the words uttered by the sage (_giraṃ munisamīritām_), those monkeys went forth (_niścakramuḥ_) from that forest; and seeing them, Vālin spoke:
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

किं भवन्तः समस्ताश्च मतङ्गवनवासिनः ।  
मत्समीपमनुप्राप्ता अपि स्वस्ति वनौकसाम् ॥ ६० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] किं भवन्तः समस्ताश्च मतङ्गवनवासिनः ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] मत्समीपमनुप्राप्ता अपि स्वस्ति वनौकसाम् ॥ ६० ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

किम् भवन्तः समस्ताः च मतंग वन वासिनः ।
मत् समीपम् अनुप्राप्ता अपि स्वस्ति वनौकसाम् ॥ ४-११-६०
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘‘मतंगवनमें निवास करनेवाले आप सभी वानर मेरे पास क्यों चले आये? वनवासियोंकी कुशल तो है न?’’ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

मतङ्ग वनदल्लि इरुव नीवॆल्ल वानररु नन्न बळिगॆ एकॆ बन्दिरुविरि? वनवासिगळॆल्लरू क्षेम ताने.॥60॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*60. matanga vana vaasinaH* = Matanga, forest, dwellers; *bhavantaH
samastaaH* = you, all; *kim mat samiipam anupraaptaa* = why, in my, presence, arrived;
*vana okasaam api svasti* = forest, dwellers, even, safeness [is there or not.]

**अनुवादः**

" 'Why all of you dwellers of Matanga forest arrived in my presence, even so, are
you the dwellers of that forest safe?' Thus Vali asked all. [4-11-60]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘Why have all of you dwellers of Mataṅga&#x27;s forest (_mataṅgavanavāsinaḥ_) come together into my presence? Is all well (_api svasti_) with the forest-dwellers?’
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Learning of the curse and spurned when he implores Mataṅga's forgiveness, Vālin dreads even to look upon Mount Ṛṣyamūka, where Sugrīva now points out Dundubhi's mountain-like skeleton.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ततस्ते कारणं सर्वं तथा शापं च वालिनः ।  
शशंसुर्वानराः सर्वे वालिने हेममालिने ॥ ६१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] ततस्ते कारणं सर्वं तथा शापं च वालिनः ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] शशंसुर्वानराः सर्वे वालिने हेममालिने ॥ ६१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

ततः ते कारणम् सर्वम् तथा शापम् च वालिनः ।
शशंसुर् वानराः सर्वे वलिने हेममालिने ॥ ४-११-६१
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘तब उन सभी वानरोंने सुवर्णमालाधारी वालीसे अपने आनेका सब कारण बताया तथा जो वालीको शाप हुआ था, उसे भी कह सुनाया ॥ ६१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

आ ऎल्ल वानररु सुवर्ण मालाधारी वालियल्लि तावु बन्द कारणवन्नु तिळिदरु. वालिगॆ उण्टाद शापवन्नु तिळिसिदरु.॥61॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*61. tataH* = then; *sarve te vaanaraaH* = all, of those,
monkeys; *sarvam kaaraNam* = all, reasons; *tathaa* = likewise; *vaalinaH shaapam
ca* = to Vali, curse, also; *valine hema maaline* = to Vali, golden, with chain [on his
chest]; *shashamsuH* = reported.

**अनुवादः**

"Then all of those monkeys have reported to Vali, the one with a golden
chest-pendant, all the reasons for their exit, likewise the curse to Vali. [4-11-61]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Then all those monkeys reported (_śaśaṃsuḥ_) to Vālin, wearer of the golden garland (_hemamāline_), the entire cause (_kāraṇaṃ sarvam_) as well as the curse upon Vālin.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

एतच्छ्रुत्वा तदा वाली वचनं वानरेरितम् ।  
स महर्षिं समासाद्य याचते स्म कृताञ्जलिः ॥ ६२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] एतच्छ्रुत्वा तदा वाली वचनं वानरेरितम् ।  
स महर्षिं समासाद्य याचते स्म कृताञ्जलिः ॥ ६२ ॥
_________________
एतच्व्रुत्वा तदा वाली वचनं वानरेरितम् ।  
स महर्षिं समासाद्य याचते स्म कृताञ्जलिः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

एतत् श्रुत्वा तदा वाली वचनम् वनर ईरितम् ।
स महर्षिम् समासाद्य याचते स्म कृत अंजलिः ॥ ४-११-६२
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘वानरोंकी कही हुई यह बात सुनकर वाली महर्षि मतंगके पास गया और हाथ जोड़कर क्षमा-याचना करने लगा ॥ ६२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

वानरु हेळिद ई मातन्नु केळि वालियु महर्षि मतङ्गर बळिगॆ होगि कैमुगिदु क्षमॆयाचिसतॊडगिदनु.॥62॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*62. tataH* = then; *saH vaalii* = he, that Vali; *vanara
iiritam* = by monkeys, uttered; *etat vacanam shrutvaa* = those, words, on hearing;
*kR^ita anjaliH maharSim sam aasaadya* = making, folded palms, that great sage, on
approaching; *yaacate sma* = begged, he has.

**अनुवादः**

"Then on hearing all those words narrated by monkeys Vali approached that great sage
and begged of him on becoming humble with palm-fold. [4-11-62]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Hearing that report uttered by the monkeys at that time, Vālin approached the great sage and implored him with folded hands (_yācate sma kṛtāñjaliḥ_).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

महर्षिस्तमनादृत्य प्रविवेशाश्रमं प्रति ।  
शापधारणभीतस्तु वाली विह्वलतां गतः ॥ ६३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] महर्षिस्तमनादृत्य प्रविवेशाश्रमं प्रति ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] शापधारणभीतस्तु वाली विह्वलतां गतः ॥ ६३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

महर्षिः तम् अनादृत्य प्रविवेश आश्रमम् प्रति ।
शाप धारण भीतः तु वाली विह्वलताम् गतः ॥ ४-११-६३
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘किंतु महर्षिने उसका आदर नहीं किया । वे चुपचाप अपने आश्रममें चले गये । इधर वाली शाप प्राप्त होनेसे भयभीत हो बहुत ही व्याकुल हो गया ॥ ६३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

आदरॆ महर्षियु अवनन्नु आदरिसलिल्ल. अवरु मौनवागि तम्म आश्रमदॊळगॆ हॊरटुहोदरु. इत्त वालियु शापग्रस्तनादुदरिन्द भयगॊण्डु बहळ व्याकुलनादनु.॥63॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*63. maharSiH tam an aadR^itya* = great sage, him, not, considering;
*pravivesha aashramam prati* = entered - withdrew, hermitage, into; *vaalii* = Vali;
*shaapa dhaaraNa bhiitaH tu* = curse, to bear, fearing for; vihvalataam gataH, distraught,
went into.

**अनुवादः**

"The sage inconsiderate of Vali's request withdrew into hermitage, and fearing to
bear the brunt of the curse Vali was distraught and withdrew from that place. [4-11-63]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Disregarding (_anādṛtya_) him, the great sage withdrew into his hermitage; and Vālin, terrified of incurring the curse (_śāpadhāraṇabhītaḥ_), fell into utter agitation (_vihvalatāṃ gataḥ_).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

ततः शापभयाद् भीतो ऋष्यमूकं महागिरिम् ।  
प्रवेष्टुं नेच्छति हरिर्द्रष्टुं वापि नरेश्वर ॥ ६४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ततः शापभयाद् भीतो ऋष्यमूकं महागिरिम् ।  
प्रवेष्टुं नेच्छति हरिर्द्रष्टुं वापि नरेश्वर ॥ ६४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

ततः शाप भयात् भीत ऋश्यमूकम् महागिरिम् ।
प्रवेष्टुम् न इच्छति हरिः द्रष्टुम् वा अपि नरेश्वर ॥ ४-११-६४
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘नरेश्वर! तबसे उस शापके भयसे डरा हुआ वाली इस महान् पर्वत ऋष्यमूकके स्थानोंमें न तो कभी प्रवेश करना चाहता है और न इस पर्वतको देखना ही चाहता है ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

नरेश्वरने! अन्दिनिन्द आ शापद भयदिन्द हॆदरिद वालियु ई महान् ऋष्यमूकवन्नु ऎन्दू प्रवेशिसलु बयसिल्ल, नोडलू कूडा बयसुवुदिल्ल.॥64॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*64. tataH* = from then; *shaapa bhayaat bhiitaH* = then, by
curse, fearing, who is dreaded; *hariH* = monkey -Vali; *R^ishyamuukam mahaa girim* =
into Rishyamuka, great, mountain; *praveSTum* = to enter; *nara iishvara* = oh,
people's, lord Rama; *draSTum vaa api* = to look at, or, even; *na icChati* = not,
wishes to.

**अनुवादः**

"Then, dreaded by the fear of curse that monkey Vali does not aspire to enter the
great mountain Rishyamuka, oh, people's lord, Rama, or he does not even wish to look at it." Thus
Sugreeva continued his narration. [4-11-64]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Thenceforth, terrified by fear of the curse (_śāpabhayād bhītaḥ_), the monkey does not wish to enter the great mountain Ṛṣyamūka, or even to look upon it (_draṣṭuṃ vāpi_), O lord of men!
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तस्याप्रवेशं ज्ञात्वाहमिदं राम महावनम् ।  
विचरामि सहामात्यो विषादेन विवर्जितः ॥ ६५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तस्याप्रवेशं ज्ञात्वाहमिदं राम महावनम् ।  
विचरामि सहामात्यो विषादेन विवर्जितः ॥ ६५ ॥
_________________
तस्याप्रवेशं ज्ञात्वाहमिदं राम महावनम् ।  
विचरामि सहामात्यो विषादेन विनर्जितः ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तस्य अप्रवेशम् ज्ञात्वा अहम् इदम् राम महावनम् ।
विचरामि सह अमात्यो विषादेन विवर्जितः ॥ ४-११-६५
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘श्रीराम! यहाँ उसका प्रवेश होना असम्भव है, यह जानकर मैं अपने मन्त्रियोंके साथ इस महान् वनमें विषाद-शून्य होकर विचरता हूँ ॥ ६५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

श्रीरामा! इल्लि अवनु प्रवेशिसुवुदु असम्भववागिदॆ. इदन्नु तिळिदु नानु नन्न मन्त्रिगळॊन्दिगॆ ई महावनदल्लि विषाद शून्यनागि सञ्चरिसुत्तिद्देनॆ.॥65॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*65. aham* = I; *tasya a pravesham j~naatvaa* = his, non,
entering, knowing; *viSaadena vi varjitaH* = of agony, got rid of; *saha amaatyaH* =
with, ministers; *idam mahaa vanam* = this, great forest; *raama* = Rama; *vi
caraami* = I am moving.

**अनुवादः**

"Knowing the inaccessibility of this forest to him I got rid of my agony, Rama, and
I move here about in this great forest along with my ministers. [4-11-65]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Knowing his non-entry (_tasyāpraveśam_), O Rāma, I roam this vast forest together with my ministers, free from despondency (_viṣādena vivarjitaḥ_).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

एषोऽस्थिनिचयस्तस्य दुन्दुभेः सम्प्रकाशते ।  
वीर्योत्सेकान्निरस्तस्य गिरिकूटनिभो महान् ॥ ६६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एषोऽस्थिनिचयस्तस्य दुन्दुभेः सम्प्रकाशते ।  
वीर्योत्सेकान्निरस्तस्य गिरिकूटनिभो महान् ॥ ६६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

एषो अस्थिनिचयः तस्य दुन्दुभेः संप्रकाशते ।
वीर्य उत्सेकात् निरस्तस्य गिरि कूट निभो महान् ॥ ४-११-६६
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘यह रहा दुन्दुभिकी हड्डियोंका ढेर, जो एक महान् पर्वतशिखरके समान जान पड़ता है । वालीने अपने बलके घमंडमें आकर दुन्दुभिके शरीरको इतनी दूर फेंका था ॥ ६६ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

इदु नोडु, दुन्दुभिय ऎलुबिन राशि, अदॊन्दु महापर्वत दन्तॆ काणुत्तिदॆ. वालियु तन्न उद्धटतनदिन्द दुन्दुभिय शरीरवन्नु इष्टु दूर ऎसिदिद्दनु.॥66॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*66. viirya utsekaat nirastasya* = by valour's, vanity, hurled;
*tasya dundubheH* = his, Dundubhi's; *giri kuuTa nibhaH* = mountain's, peak, in
similarity; *mahaan eSaH asthi nicayaH* = huge one, this is, bones, heap; *sam
prakaashate* = shining forth.

**अनुवादः**

"This huge heap of bones that is shining forth like a mountaintop is that of
Dundubhi, which Vali once hurled by the vanity of his valour. [4-11-66]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

And here gleams (_samprakāśate_) the vast, mountain-peak-like (_girikūṭanibhaḥ_) heap of bones (_asthinicayaḥ_) of that Dundubhi, who was cast down through arrogance over his valor (_vīryotsekān nirastasya_)!
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Pointing to the seven massive sāla trees that Vālin could strip of leaves, Sugrīva asks how Rāma can slay him, and proposes two tests when Lakṣmaṇa smiles.
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

इमे च विपुलाः सालाः सप्त शाखावलम्बिनः ।  
यत्रैकं घटते वाली निष्पत्रयितुमोजसा ॥ ६७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

इमे च विपुलाः सालाः सप्त शाखावलम्बिनः ।  
यत्रैकं घटते वाली निष्पत्रयितुमोजसा ॥ ६७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

इमे च विपुलाः सालाः सप्त शाखा अवलंबिनः ।
यत्र एकम् घटते वाली निष् पत्रयितुम् ओजसा ॥ ४-११-६७
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘ये सात सालके विशाल एवं मोटे वृक्ष हैं, जो अनेक उत्तम शाखाओंसे सुशोभित होते हैं । वाली इनमेंसे एक-एकको बलपूर्वक हिलाकर पत्रहीन कर सकता है ॥ ६७ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

इवु एळु विशालवाद, दप्पवाद सालवृक्षगळिवॆ, अवु उत्तम रॆम्बॆगळिन्द सुशोभितवागिवॆ. वालियु इवुगळल्लि ऒन्दॊन्दन्नु बलवागि अल्लाडिसि ऎलॆगळिल्लदन्तॆ माडुत्तिद्दनु.॥67॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*67. shaakhaa avalambinaH* = branches, dangling [full with]; *ime
sapta vipulaaH saalaaH* = these, seven, enormous, sala trees; *yatra ekam* = among whom,
one - one at a time; vaalii ojasaa Vali, by his vigour; *niS patrayitum* = without, leaves
to make it; *ghaTate* = will be trying, or he is capable.

**अनुवादः**

"Also these are the seven enormous sala trees full with their branches, and Vali is
capable to make each of them leafless by his vigour, of course, one at a time. [4-11-67]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

And here stand seven huge _sāla_ trees (_vipulāḥ sālāḥ sapta_) with low-hanging branches (_śākhāvalambinaḥ_), among which Vālin is able by his sheer strength (_ojasā_) to strip a single one of its leaves (_niṣpatrayitum_)!
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

एतदस्यासमं वीर्यं मया राम प्रकाशितम् ।  
कथं तं वालिनं हन्तुं समरे शक्ष्यसे नृप ॥ ६८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एतदस्यासमं वीर्यं मया राम प्रकाशितम् ।  
कथं तं वालिनं हन्तुं समरे शक्ष्यसे नृप ॥ ६८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

एतत् अस्य असमम् वीर्यम् मया राम प्रकाशितम् ।
कथम् तम् वालिनम् हन्तुम् समरे शक्ष्यसे नृप ॥ ४-११-६८
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘श्रीराम! यह मैंने वालीके अनुपम पराक्रमको प्रकाशित किया है । नरेश्वर! आप उस वालीको समराङ्गणमें कैसे मार सकेंगे’ ॥ ६८ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

श्रीरामा! ई वालिय अनुपम पराक्रमवन्नु नानु वर्णिसिरुवॆनु. नरेश्वरने! नीनु आ वालियन्नु समराङ्गणदल्लि हेगॆ कॊल्लबल्लॆ.॥68॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*68. raama* = oh, Rama; *asya a samam* = his, un, equalled;
*etat viiryam mayaa prakaashitam* = all this, his vitality, by me, apprised; *nR^ipa*
= oh, king; *samare tam vaalinam hantum* = such a, Vali, to kill; *katham shakSyase*
= how, is it possible for you.

**अनुवादः**

"Oh, Rama, I am apprising all this to tell about the unequalled vitality of Vali,
and oh, king, then how is it possible for you to eliminate Vali in war." Thus Sugreeva enquired
with Rama. [4-11-68]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

This matchless valor (_asamaṃ vīryam_) of his has now been set forth by me, O Rāma; how then will you be able to slay (_hantuṃ śakṣyase_) that Vālin in battle, O king?’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तथा ब्रुवाणं सुग्रीवं प्रहसल्ँ लक्ष्मणोऽब्रवीत् ।  
कस्मिन् कर्मणि निर्वृत्ते श्रद्दध्या वालिनो वधम् ॥ ६९ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तथा ब्रुवाणं सुग्रीवं प्रहसल्ँ लक्ष्मणोऽब्रवीत् ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] कस्मिन् कर्मणि निर्वृत्ते श्रद्दध्या वालिनो वधम् ॥ ६९ ॥
_________________
तथा ब्रुवाणं सुग्रीवं प्रहसल्लक्ष्मणोऽब्रवीत् ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] कस्मिन्कर्मणि निर्वृत्ते श्रद्दध्या वालिनो वधम् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तथा बृउवाणम् सुग्रीवम् प्रहसन् लक्ष्मणो अब्रवीत् ।
कस्मिन् कर्मणि निर्वृत्ते श्रद्दध्या वालिनः वधम् ॥ ४-११-६९
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

सुग्रीवके ऐसा कहनेपर लक्ष्मणको बड़ी हँसी आयी । वे हँसते हुए ही बोले—‘कौन-सा काम कर देनेपर तुम्हें विश्वास होगा कि श्रीरामचन्द्रजी वालीका वध कर सकेंगे’ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

सुग्रीवनु हीगॆ हेळिदाग लक्ष्मणाग्रजनिगॆ नगुबन्तु. याव कार्य माडिबिट्टाग श्रीरामचन्द्रनु वालियन्नु वधिसबल्ल ऎम्ब विश्वास उण्टादीतु; ऎन्दु मुगुळ्नगुत्ता केळिदनु.॥69॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*69. lakshmaNaH* = Lakshmana; *tathaa bR^iuvaaNam sugriivam* =
thus, speaking, to Sugreeva; *prahasan abraviit* = smilingly, said; *kasmin karmaNi
nirvR^itte* = which, act, on performing; *vaalinaH vadham shraddadhyaa* = Vali's,
killing, you have confidence.

**अनुवादः**

When Sugreeva spoke that way Lakshmana a little smiled and asked him, "On performing
which act do you confide in the possibility of Vali's elimination?" [4-11-69]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Smiling (_prahasan_) at Sugrīva as he spoke thus, Lakṣmaṇa said: ‘Upon the accomplishment of what deed (_kasmin karmaṇi nirvṛtte_) would you trust (_śraddadhyāḥ_) in the slaying of Vālin?’
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तमुवाचाथ सुग्रीवः सप्त सालानिमान् पुरा ।  
एवमेकैकशो वाली विव्याधाथ स चासकृत् ॥ ७० ॥  
रामो निर्दारयेदेषां बाणेनैकेन च द्रुमम् ।  
वालिनं निहतं मन्ये दृष्ट्वा रामस्य विक्रमम् ॥ ७१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] तमुवाचाथ सुग्रीवः सप्त सालानिमान् पुरा ।

[^non-baroDa-CE] रामो निर्दारयेदेषां बाणेनैकेन च द्रुमम् ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] वालिनं निहतं मन्ये दृष्ट्वा रामस्य विक्रमम् ॥ ७१ ॥

[^non-baroDa-CE] तमुवाचाथ सुग्रीवः सप्त सालानिमान्पुरा ।  
एवमेकैकशो वालीविव्याथाथ स चासकृत् ॥
रामोऽपि निर्दारयेदेषां बाणेनैकेन च द्रुमम् ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] वालिनं निहतं मन्ये दृष्ट्वा रामस्य विक्रमम् ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

तम् उवाचथ सुग्रीवः सप्त सालन् इमान् पुरा ।
एवम् एकैकशो वाली विव्याथ अथ स असकृत् ॥ ४-११-७०
रामो निर्दारयेद् एषाम् बाणेन एकेन च द्रुमम् ।
वालिनम् निहतम् मन्ये दृष्ट्वा रामस्य विक्रमम् ॥ ४-११-७१
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

तब सुग्रीवने उनसे कहा— ‘पूर्वकालमें वालीने सालके इन सातों वृक्षोंको एक-एक करके कई बार बींध डाला है । अतः श्रीरामचन्द्रजी भी यदि इनमेंसे किसी एक वृक्षको एक ही बाणसे छेद डालेंगे तो इनका पराक्रम देखकर मुझे वालीके मारे जानेका विश्वास हो जायगा ॥ ७०-७१ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

आग सुग्रीवनु - ‘हिन्दॆ वालियु ई एळु साल वृक्षगळन्नु ऒन्दॊन्दागि अनेक बारि सीळि हाकिरुवनु. आद्दरिन्द श्रीरामने इदरल्लि यावुदादरू ऒन्दु वृक्षवन्नु ऒन्दे बाणदिन्द कत्तरिसिबिट्टरॆ, निन्न पराक्रम नोडि ननगॆ वालियन्नु कॊल्लुवि ऎम्ब विश्वास उण्टादीतु’ ऎन्दु हेळिदनु.॥70-71॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*70. tataH* = then; *sugriivaH tam uvaachatha* = Sugreeva, to
him, said; *puraa saH vaalii* = earlier, he, Vali; *imaan sapta saalan* = these,
seven, sala trees; *evam* = that way; *eka ekashaH* = one, after other; *atha
asakR^it* = that way, on many occasions; *vi vyaatha* = agitated them.

**अनुवादः**

Then Sugreeva said to Lakshmana, "earlier Vali used to agitate each of the trees,
one after the other, on many occasions." [4-11-70]

**टिप्पनी**

Here some translations say that Vali pierced one arrow without using bow in one tree, or in leaves,
then removing that arrow again pierced, and gone on piercing for many times hurting the trees with many
holes. While some more translations contain the meaning, taking the clue from *niSpatraa kurute*
or *niSpatraayitum ghaTate* as in verse 67 above, meaning it to be, 'aimed arrow and pierced
the trees and when the arrow exited out of the tree, made its fin-feathers to come out.' In either way,
'arrow' has no place in monkey's hands. The very birth of this monkey race is to fight with their nails,
teeth, and tree-trunks and branches. Hence, the word *vivyaadha* means that Vali tortures the
trees by shaking their trunks, and shattering all the leaves in his show of strength.
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*71. raamaH* = Rama; *eSaam drumam* = among them, one tree;
*ekena baaNena nirdaarayet* = with one, arrows, if rends; *raamasya vikramam
dR^iSTvaa* = Rama's, valour, on seeing; *vaalinam nihatam manye* = Vali is, utterly
dead, I construe.

**अनुवादः**

"If Rama can rend one tree out of the seven with only one arrow, then on seeing
Rama's valour I can construe that Vali is utterly dead at his hand. [4-11-71]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

**70:** Then Sugrīva said to him: ‘Of old, Vālin repeatedly (_asakṛt_) shook and pierced (_vivyātha_) these seven _sāla_ trees one by one (_ekaikaśaḥ_) in just this manner.

**71:** Should Rāma cleave (_nirdārayet_) even a single tree among these with a single arrow (_bāṇenaikena_), then upon beholding Rāma&#x27;s valor I would reckon Vālin already slain (_nihatam manye_)!
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

हतस्य महिषस्यास्थि पादेनैकेन लक्ष्मण ।  
उद्यम्य प्रक्षिपेच्चापि तरसा द्वे धनुःशते ॥ ७२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] हतस्य महिषस्यास्थि पादेनैकेन लक्ष्मण ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] उद्यम्य प्रक्षिपेच्चापि तरसा द्वे धनुःशते ॥ ७२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

हतस्य महिषस्य अस्थि पादेन एकेन लक्ष्मण ।
उद्यम्य प्रक्षिपेत् च अपि तरसा द्वे धनुः शते ॥ ४-११-७२
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘लक्ष्मण! यदि इस महिषरूपधारी दुन्दुभिकी हड्डीको एक ही पैरसे उठाकर बलपूर्वक दो सौ धनुषकी दूरीपर फेंक सकें तो भी मैं यह मान लूँगा कि इनके हाथसे वालीका वध हो सकता है’ ॥ ७२ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

लक्ष्मण! ई महिषरूपधारी दुन्दुभिय ऎलुबुगळन्नु ऒन्दु कालिनिन्द ऎत्ति इन्नूरु धनुस्सु दूरक्कॆ बलवागि ऎसॆयबल्लनादरॆ, इवन कैयिन्द वालिय वधॆयागबल्लदु ऎन्दु नानु ऒप्पिकॊळ्ळुवॆनु.॥72॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*72. lakshmaNa* = oh, Lakshmana; *hatasya mahiSasya asthi* =
dead, buffalo's, bone [skeleton]; *ekena paadena udyamya* = by one, foot, lifting;
*tarasaa dve shate dhanuH* = with might, two, hundred, bow [lengths, two hundred
bow-lengths]; *prakSipet ca api* = kicks and throws, also, even.

**अनुवादः**

"Lakshmana, if he lifts and kicks the skeleton of this dead buffalo by the might of
his foot, and makes it fall at a distance of two hundred bow-lengths, I can confide." So said
Sugreeva to Lakshmana. [4-11-72]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

And, O Lakṣmaṇa, should he with a single foot (_pādenaikena_) lift up the skeleton of this slain buffalo and hurl (_prakṣipec cāpi_) it with force two hundred bow-lengths (_dve dhanuḥśate_)!’
</details>
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः - अवतारिका</summary>

<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Reflecting for a moment and fearing lest Rāma take offense, Sugrīva extols Vālin's invincibility even against the gods and declares that he has sought refuge in Rāma like Himavān.
</details>
</details>

एवमुक्त्वा तु सुग्रीवो रामं रक्तान्तलोचनम् ।  
ध्यात्वा मुहूर्तं काकुत्स्थं पुनरेव वचोऽब्रवीत् ॥ ७३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] एवमुक्त्वा तु सुग्रीवो रामं रक्तान्तलोचनम् ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] ध्यात्वा मुहूर्तं काकुत्स्थं पुनरेव वचोऽब्रवीत् ॥ ७३ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

एवम् उक्त्वा तु सुग्रीवो रामम् रक्तान्त लोचनम् ।
ध्यत्वा मुहूर्तम् काकुत्स्थम् पुनरेव वचो अब्रवीत् ॥ ४-११-७३
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

जिनके नेत्रप्रान्त कुछ-कुछ लाल थे, उन श्रीरामसे ऐसा कहकर सुग्रीव दो घड़ीतक कुछ सोच-विचारमें पड़े रहे । इसके बाद वे ककुत्स्थकुलभूषण श्रीरामसे फिर बोले—
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

स्वल्प कॆम्पाद नेत्रगळुळ्ळ श्रीरामनल्लि हीगॆ हेळि सुग्रीवनु ऎरडु गळिगॆ एनो योचनॆयल्लि मुळुगिदनु. बळिक अवनु ककुत्स्थकुलभूषण श्रीरामनल्लि पुनः हेळिदनु.॥73॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*73. sugriivaH* = Sugreeva; *rakta anta lochanam raamam* = to
red, corner, eyed, to Rama; *evam uktvaa* = thus, when said; *muhuurtam dhyatvaa* =
for a awhile, on thinking; *punaH eva kaakutstham vachaH abraviit* = again, thus, to Rama,
words, spoke.

**अनुवादः**

Sugreeva paused for a while on saying thus to Rama, for Rama's eye-corners are
reddened with anger towards Vali, and then Sugreeva again spoke to Rama. [4-11-73]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

Having spoken thus to Rāma, whose eyes were reddish at the corners (_raktāntalocanam_), Sugrīva reflected (_dhyātvā_) for a moment and once again addressed Kākutstha:
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

शूरश्च शूरमानी च प्रख्यातबलपौरुषः ।  
बलवान् वानरो वाली संयुगेष्वपराजितः ॥ ७४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

[^non-baroDa-CE] शूरश्च शूरमानी च प्रख्यातबलपौरुषः ।  
[^non-baroDa-CE] बलवान् वानरो वाली संयुगेष्वपराजितः ॥ ७४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम् - VR</summary>

शूरः च शूरमानी च प्रख्यात बल पौरुषः ।
बलवान् वानरः वाली संयुगेषु अपराजितः ॥ ४-११-७४
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (हिन्दी)</summary>

‘वाली शूर है और स्वयं भी उसे अपने शौर्यपर अभिमान है । उसके बल और पुरुषार्थ विख्यात हैं । वह बलवान् वानर अबतकके युद्धोंमें कभी पराजित नहीं हुआ है ॥ ७४ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>अनुवाद (कन्नड)</summary>

वालियु शूरनागिद्दानॆ मत्तु स्वतः तन्न शौर्यद मेलॆ अभिमानपडुत्तानॆ. अवन बल हागू पौरुष विख्यातवागिदॆ. आ बलवन्त वानरनु युद्धदल्लि ऎन्दू पराजितनागलिल्ल.॥74॥
</details>

<details><summary>Desiraju Hanumanta Rao</summary>

**पदच्छेदः**

*74. shuuraH ca shuura maanii ca* = intrepid, also, his intrepidity, he
esteems, also; *prakhyaata bala pauruSaH* = well, renowned, by might, tenacity; *balavaan
vaanaraH vaalii* = mighty one, monkey, Vali; *samyugeSu a paraajitaH* = in combats, not,
defeated.

**अनुवादः**

"Vali is a mighty monkey, an intrepid one, who esteems his own intrepidity, and one
who is well renowned by his might and tenacity, and in combats he is an undefeated one.
[4-11-74]
</details>


<details><summary>English (Gemini 4, itihasa.io)</summary>

‘The monkey Vālin is a hero (_śūraḥ_), esteemed by heroes (_śūramānī_), of renowned strength and prowess (_prakhyātabalapauruṣaḥ_), mighty, and undefeated in battles (_saṃyugeṣv aparājitaḥ_).
</details>
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

दृश्यन्ते चास्य कर्माणि दुष्कराणि सुरैरपि ।  
यानि सञ्चिन्त्य भीतोऽहमृष्यमूकमुपाश्रितः ॥ ७५ ॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>
